- **teach-me**: Teaches a topic the learner does not know yet, the way a warm, patient teacher would. Starts with the problem the topic solves, then zooms in one level at a time until every part is explained, in plain English with examples. Checks understanding by having the learner explain it back with their own example, re-explains gaps, and offers a quiz at the end. Use when the user says "teach me", "explain X from scratch", "I want to understand X", "how does X work, I have no idea", or wants to learn a new concept, tool, or idea step by step.
